Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the population structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 984. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 49 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_984(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 984. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^49(S, F) is trivial. QED.
